# ADCIRC fort.* files — purpose, I/O, and connectivity

## Scope note

This note maps every standard `fort.NN` filename in an ADCIRC run to:
- **purpose** (what the file holds),
- **I/O direction** (input / output / both),
- **reader/writer subroutine** with `file:line` from the v55 source tree,
- **manual reference** (ADCIRC Wiki / official PDFs) where the format/parameters are documented,
- **dependencies** (which other `fort.*` files or fort.15 controls drive it).

Coverage focuses on the main solver path under `models/adcirc/source_code/adcirc/{src,prep,util,wind}/`. Parallel-only artifacts (per-PE `fort.18`, MPI staging) and clearly deprecated units (e.g., legacy `fort.33` solver log) are noted but not detailed.

## Source basis

- **Source-code map** — built 2026-05-05 by scanning `OPEN/READ/WRITE/CLOSE` statements across the main solver tree via Codex (`gpt-5.3-codex`). Cited as `[file=src/... line=N]`.
- **Manual cross-reference** — retrieved via rag_proxy against the `manuals` collection filtered to `model=adcirc` (15 K chunks: official PDFs, ADCIRC Wiki, workshop materials). Cited as `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.NN_file]` or `[file=pdf:adcirc:<stem> page=N]`.

Both source and manual citations are preserved per claim. When the two disagree, the source code is treated as ground truth.

## Why this note exists

Every ADCIRC run reads at least `fort.14` + `fort.15` and writes a subset of `fort.61–93` according to flags inside `fort.15`. The full mapping is fragmented across the User Manual, the Theory document, and dozens of wiki pages. A single reference (with file:line citations into the actual reader) lets future debugging skip the manual scan and go straight to the right subroutine.

## File inventory

### Required input files

| fort# | Purpose | Reader | Source citation | Manual reference |
|-------|---------|--------|-----------------|------------------|
| **14** | Mesh geometry: nodes, elements, boundary conditions | `read_global` / `readMesh` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=380]`, `[file=prep/read_global.F line=96]` | `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.14_file]` — "contains the grid coordinates, element identifiers and boundary conditions for the model run" |
| **15** | Master control: physics flags (NOLIBF/NOLIFA/NOLICA), forcing selectors (NWS, NTIP), output specifiers (NOUT*) | `READ_INPUT`, `READ_INPUT_3D` | `[file=src/read_input.F line=394]`, `[file=src/read_input.F line=5296]` | `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.15_file]`; key parameters at `[file=wiki:adcirc:NOLIBF]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:NOLIFA]`, `[file=wiki:adcirc:NWS]` |

### Optional input files (selected by `fort.15` flags)

| fort# | Purpose | Activation flag (in fort.15) | Reader | Source citation | Manual reference |
|-------|---------|------------------------------|--------|-----------------|------------------|
| **13** | 노드 속성 (Manning·primitive weighting·교각·canopy 등) | `NWP > 0` | ReadNodalAttr | `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1080]` ` DO k=1,NWP `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1081]` ` READ(15,*) AttrName ` | Chezy·Manning·quadratic 마찰 속성은 NOLIBF가 1 또는 3이어야 한다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1855]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `) 현행 매개변수 문서와 코드의 주석·오류 문장은 1만 적는다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=387]` ` Parameter controlling the type of bottom stress parameterization used in a 2DDI ADCIRC run. This parameter must be specified but is ignored in a 3D run. Note: In the NWP section, if the user selects quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1848]` ` C     ERROR CHECK: If Chezy, Manning's or Quadratic friction was loaded `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1849]` ` C     from the nodal attributes file, NOLIBF must be 1. `) |
| **22** | NWS가 선택하는 기상 입력 | 기상 NWS별 조건 | NWS별 reader | `[file=src/read_input.F line=1769]` ` READ(15,FMT=*,END=99998,ERR=99999) NWS ` | NWS=12는 OWI ASCII 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=298]` ` **NWS = 12** `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=299]` ` Wind velocity (10 minute averaged winds at 10m) and atmospheric pressure are provided in the OWI format on one or two rectangular (lat/lon) grid(s). If two grids are used, the first is designated as the large ("basin") scale grid, and the second is designated as the small ("region") scale grid. The Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File (fort.22) is only used to specify a few configuration parameters, while the actual wind fields are recorded in files named: `) NWS=13은 OWI NetCDF 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=10]` ` Oceanweather Inc. (OWI) NetCDF Meteorological Input Files `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=214]` ` -  *NWS13File*: optional file name for netCDF file ( *fort.22.nc* default ) `) NCICE=12와 양수 기상 NWS=1·2·7의 조합은 호환성 검사에서 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=2322]` ` IF((NCICE.GT.0).AND. `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=2323]` ` &       ((NWS.EQ.1).OR.(NWS.EQ.2).OR.(NWS.EQ.7))) THEN `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=2328]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `) |
| **23** | 파랑 방사 응력 입력 | NRS=1 또는 2 | NRS=1: rsinit / rsget. NRS=2: RS2INIT | `[file=src/cstart.F line=312]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.1) THEN `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=313]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23') `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=317]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.2) THEN `; `[file=src/cstart.F line=318]` ` CALL RS2INIT(RSNX1,RSNY1,NP) ` | NRS=1은 노드별 응력을 읽는다. (`[file=src/wind.F line=7549]` ` read(pbljagf,'(i8,5e13.5)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) `; `[file=src/wind.F line=7550]` ` &         nhg, rsnxRaw, rsnyRaw `) NRS=2는 fort.23을 STWAVE 제어 파일로 읽는다. (`[file=src/rs2.F line=89]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23',STATUS='OLD') `; `[file=src/rs2.F line=93]` ` read(23,*,err=99999) type `; `[file=src/rs2.F line=438]` ` read(23,*,err=99999) ns    !number of stwave grid files `) 이 초기화는 NRS=3·4·5에서 fort.23을 읽지 않는다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=6]` `` The fort.23 file contains wave radiation stress data that can be used to drive ADCIRC either by itself or in combination with other forcing (including winds). This file is read when ABS(:ref:`NWS <NWS>`) >= 100 in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. The format is similar to the meteorological input file used when :ref:`NWS <NWS>` = -4 (i.e. the PBL hurricane model input format following a hot start). ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=312]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=313]` ` OPEN(23,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.23') `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=317]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.2) THEN `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=318]` ` CALL RS2INIT(RSNX1,RSNY1,NP) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1466]` ` SELECT CASE(NRS) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1467]` ` CASE(1) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1471]` ` CASE(2) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1476]` ` CASE(3,4,5) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1477]` ` ! do nothing, initialization takes place in timestep.F `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2271]` ` IF(NRS.EQ.3) THEN `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=2274]` ` &    /,9X,'WAVES WILL BE COUPLED TO SWAN!') `) |
| 23 / NRS=1·STWAVE type=2 | 노드 응력·제어·경계·방사 응력 자료 레코드 | NRS=1 또는 NRS=2, type=2 | RS2INIT / rs2get_type2 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=15]` `` :ref:`JN <JN>`, :ref:`RSX(JN) <RSX>`, :ref:`RSY(JN) <RSY>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=22]` `` 2. Radiation stresses are input directly to a subset of nodes in the ADCIRC grid (as specified by the node number :ref:`JN <JN>`). ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=26]` ` 4. Each data line must have the format I8, 2E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired. A line containing the # symbol in column 2 indicates radiation stress data at the next time increment begins on the following line. At each new time, any node that is not specified in the input file is assumed to have zero wave radiation stress. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7535]` ` rsnx(1:np) = 0.d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7536]` ` rsny(1:np) = 0.d0 `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7540]` ` read(23,'(a80)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) pbljagf `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7541]` ` if (pbljagf(1:1).eq.'#') then `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7542]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7545]` ` if(pbljagf(2:2).ne.'#') exit `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7549]` ` read(pbljagf,'(i8,5e13.5)',end=500,err=600,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7550]` ` &         nhg, rsnxRaw, rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7551]` ` rsnx(nhg) = rsnxRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7552]` ` rsny(nhg) = rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7555]` ` if (pbljagf(1:1).eq.'#') then `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7556]` ` call terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7560]` ` if(pbljagf(2:2).eq.'#') exit `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=28]` ` 5. Wave radiation stress must be input in units of velocity squared (consistent with the units of gravity). Stress in these units is obtained by dividing stress in units of force/area by the reference density of water. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2112]` ` Applied wave radiation stress in the x,y directions divided by the reference density of water (should be units (length/time)2). An oceanographic convention is used where stress is positive when it is pointed in positive coordinate directions. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7551]` ` rsnx(nhg) = rsnxRaw `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7552]` ` rsny(nhg) = rsnyRaw `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=20]` ` 1. At least two datasets must be present in the file to allow for time interpolation. If only one dataset is present, the run will terminate with an unexpected end-of-file error. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=24]` `` 3. If ADCIRC is cold started, the first set of radiation stress data corresponds to TIME=:ref:`STATIM <STATIM>`. If ADCIRC is hot started, the first set of radiation stress data corresponds to TIME=HOT START TIME. Additional sets of radiation stress data must be provided every :ref:`RSTIMINC <RSTIMINC>`, where :ref:`RSTIMINC <RSTIMINC>` is the radiation stress time interval and is specified in the :ref:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. Radiation stresses are interpolated in time to the ADCIRC time step. ``<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=310]` ` RSTIME1 = STATIM*86400.D0 `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=311]` ` RSTIME2 = RSTIME1+RSTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=314]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX1,RSNY1) `<br>`[file=src/cstart.F line=315]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX2,RSNY2) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1464]` ` RSTIME1 = TimeLoc `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1465]` ` RSTIME2 = RSTIME1 + RSTIMINC `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1469]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX1,RSNY1) `<br>`[file=src/hstart.F line=1470]` ` CALL RSGET(RSNX2,RSNY2) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort23.rst line=30]` ` 6. Data must be provided for the entire model run, otherwise the run will crash! `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7566]` ` 500   call allMessage(ERROR,'Unexpectedly reached end-of-file.') ! (`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=3]` ` Fort.25, 255/277: Ice Coverage Input Files `; `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=132]` ` OPEN(225,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.225', STATUS='OLD') `; `[file=src/owi_ice.F line=158]` ` OPEN(227,FILE=TRIM(GBLINPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.227',STATUS='OLD') `) `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=8]` ` In order to use ice field data, two changes are required to an existing fort.15: the ten thousands place must be set to 12 (see the NWS Simplified Table of Values for details), and the time increment of the ice field data (CICE_TIMINC) in seconds must be supplied on the WTIMINC line (see the Supplemental Meteorology/Wave/Ice Parameters table for details). Ice coverage datasets are linearly interpolated in time by ADCIRC, as a result, at least two datasets are required. `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1775]` ` NCICE = INT(ABS(NWS)/1000) `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=1776]` ` NWS = INT(ABS(NWS)-NCICE*1000)*INT(NWS/ABS(NWS))  !RESETTING NWS/NRS `<br>문서는 -1.0%를 0.0%로 처리한다고 적는다. 코드는 보간 셀의 음수 모서리 값을 비음수 모서리 평균으로 바꾼다. 비음수 모서리가 없으면 코드는 CVBAR의 초기값 0을 그대로 사용한다(`src/owi_ice.F:371` `CVbar = 0.d0`, `src/owi_ice.F:374` `if(CV(k) .ge. 0.d0) then`, `src/owi_ice.F:379` `if (ibar.gt.0) CVbar = CVbar/real(ibar,8)`). [10-12 전수 검증 정정] `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort25.rst line=21]` ` If numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 1, only the fort.225 file will be read; if numIceFields in the fort.25 file is set to 2 then both the fort.225 and fort.227 files will be read. The fort.225 (the basin scale, i.e., large scale coarse grid) and optional fort.227 (the region scale, i.e., smaller scale finer grid) files contain the actual ice percentage values (0.0 to 100.0%) and a value of -1.0 which means land or no data available. A value of -1.0% is treated as 0.0% ice in ADCIRC. `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=367]` ` CV(1) = CiceB(xi,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=368]` ` CV(2) = CiceB(xi+1,yi) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=369]` ` CV(3) = CiceB(xi+1,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=370]` ` CV(4) = CiceB(xi,yi+1) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=374]` ` if(CV(k) .ge. 0.d0) then `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=375]` ` CVbar = CVbar + CV(k) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=376]` ` ibar = ibar + 1 `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=379]` ` if (ibar.gt.0) CVbar = CVbar/real(ibar,8) `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=381]` ` if (CV(k).lt.0.d0) CV(k) = CVbar `<br>`[file=src/owi_ice.F line=385]` ` Cice1=w1*CV(1)+w2*CV(2)+w3*CV(3)+w4*CV(4) ` |

### Output files — 2D station / global

Activation: each is gated by a `NOUT*` flag in `fort.15` (e.g., `NOUTE`, `NOUTGE`, `NOUTV`).

| fort# | Purpose | Flag | Writer | Source citation | Manual reference |
|-------|---------|------|--------|-----------------|------------------|
| **61** | Elevation time series at recording stations | `NOUTE`, `NSTAE` | `initOutput2D` / `writeOutput2D` (also reopened in cold/hot start) | `[file=src/write_output.F line=510]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=376]` | `[file=wiki:adcirc:Fort.61_file]` |
| **62** | Velocity time series at recording stations | `NOUTV`, `NSTAV` | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=546]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=394]` | (sibling of fort.61) |
| **63** | Elevation field at all nodes | `NOUTGE` | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=598]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=487]` | (mesh from `fort.14`) |
| **64** | 2D depth-averaged velocity field at all nodes | `NOUTGV` | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=710]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=584]` | — |
| **75** | Time-varying bathymetry field output | `TimeBathyControl` namelist | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=946]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=467]` | — |
| **81** | Concentration time series at stations | `NOUTC` (with `IM=10`) | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=1160]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=412]` | — |
| **83** | Concentration field at all nodes | `NOUTGC` | same family | `[file=src/write_output.F line=1191]`, `[file=src/cstart.F line=665]` | — |

### Output files — meteorological

농도 전역 출력 NOUTGC의 파일명은 fort.83이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1213]` ` ConcDescript % lun                  = 83 `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1214]` ` ConcDescript % specifier            = NOUTGC `)
fort.77 헤더의 자료 세트 수는 ndsets_tvw이다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=1188]` ` write(lun, 1010) ndsets_tvw, size_this, dtdp*nspool_tvw, `; `[file=src/globalio.F line=1189]` ` &                 nspool_tvw, 1, FileFmtVersion `)
fort.72의 실제 보간 대상은 WVNXOUT·WVNYOUT이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=807]` ` WindVelStaDescript % interped_array       => wvnxout `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=808]` ` WindVelStaDescript % interped_array2      => wvnyout `)
fort.83의 ASCII 값은 CH1이다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=1219]` ` ConcDescript % array                => CH1 `)

## Connectivity graph

```
                    ┌────────────────────┐
                    │  fort.15 (control) │
                    │  - NWS, NWP, NTIP  │
                    │  - NOUT* flags     │
                    │  - harmonic setup  │
                    └────────┬───────────┘
                             │ drives
        ┌────────────┬───────┴────────┬──────────────┐
        ▼            ▼                ▼              ▼
   fort.14       fort.13         fort.22        fort.23/24/25
   (mesh)        (nodal attr)    (met forcing)  (waves / SAL / ice)
        │            │                │              │
        └────────────┴────────────────┴──────────────┘
                     │
                     ▼
              ADCIRC main loop
                     │
        ┌────────────┼────────────┬──────────────┬───────────────┐
        ▼            ▼            ▼              ▼               ▼
   fort.61–65   fort.71–74   fort.41–42    fort.51–53      fort.81/83/90/92
   (2D out)     (met out)    (3D station) (harmonic)     (concentration/tau0/sponge)
                     │
                     ▼
              fort.80 metadata
              merges per-PE outputs
              after parallel run
```

## Decision Guide

- 일반 수위·유속·농도 출력은 NOUT 계열을 descriptor에 연결한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=512]` ` ElevStaDescript % specifier            = NOUTE `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=548]` ` VelStaDescript % specifier            = NOUTV `; `[file=src/write_output.F line=1214]` ` ConcDescript % specifier            = NOUTGC `)
- NHSTAR는 핫스타트 출력 형식을 선택한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4522]` ` READ(15,*) NHSTAR,NHSINC `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4535]` ` SELECT CASE(NHSTAR) `)
- NHSINC는 핫스타트 출력 간격을 정한다. (`[file=src/timestep.F line=1196]` ` ITEST=(IT/NHSINC)*NHSINC `; `[file=src/timestep.F line=1200]` ` if ((ABS(NHSTAR) > 0 .and. ITEST == IT) .or. (-IHOT == IT)) then  !tcm v51.26 added abs(nhstar) to handle when nhstar = -1 `)
- v56.2.1은 잘못된 NHSTAR를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4553]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, message="Input value of NHSTAR is invalid.") `)
- 코드는 `(NHSINC.EQ.0).AND.(NHSTAR.NE.0)` 조건에서 종료한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=4556]` ` IF((NHSINC.EQ.0).AND.(NHSTAR.NE.0)) THEN `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=4557]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `)
- 두 실행의 NHSTAR/NHSINC 변경이 일반 출력 형식의 조용한 표류를 일으키는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- 출력 제어값의 절댓값은 형식을 선택한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3627]` ` SELECT CASE(ABS(NOUTE)) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3632]` ` CASE(1) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3637]` ` CASE(3) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3642]` ` CASE(5) `)
- v56.2.1은 NOUTE의 절댓값 2를 거부한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3634]` ` CASE(2) `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3635]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `; `[file=src/read_input.F line=3636]` ` &                     message='Binary format output is no longer supported.') `)
- 핫스타트 후 음수 제어값은 새 관측소 출력 파일을 시작한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1563]` ` NSCOUE=0 `; `[file=src/hstart.F line=1571]` ` 1041   FORMAT(//,' A NEW ELEVATION STATION FILE WILL BE STARTED') `)
- 핫스타트 후 양수 제어값은 존재하는 출력 파일에 이어 쓰는 설정이다. [10-12 전수 검증 보완] netCDF 일반 출력 파일이 없으면 비 CMPI 경로는 새 파일 생성 플래그를 설정한다(`src/netcdfio.F90:3887` `if ((descript%lun /= 67) .and. (descript%lun /= 68)) then`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3888` `if ((IHOT == 0) .or. (descript%specifier < 0) .or. &`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3889` `(myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.)) then`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3905` `#else`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3911` `if ((abs(descript%specifier) == NETCDF3) .or. &`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3912` `(abs(descript%specifier) == NETCDF4)) then`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3913` `myFile%createFile = .true.`). [10-12 전수 검증 보완] 같은 파일 부재 조건의 CMPI 경로는 오류를 기록한다(`src/netcdfio.F90:3890` `#ifdef CMPI`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3897` `if (myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.) then`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3902` `call allMessage(ERROR, scratchMessage)`, `src/netcdfio.F90:3903` `ret_error = .true.`). [10-12 전수 검증 보완] (`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=858]` ` NOUTE = 1 Output is provided at the selected elevation recording stations in standard ascii format. Following a hot start, continued output is merged into the existing fort.61 file. `)
- NWS=12는 OWI ASCII 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=298]` ` **NWS = 12** `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=299]` ` Wind velocity (10 minute averaged winds at 10m) and atmospheric pressure are provided in the OWI format on one or two rectangular (lat/lon) grid(s). If two grids are used, the first is designated as the large ("basin") scale grid, and the second is designated as the small ("region") scale grid. The Single File Meteorological Forcing Input File (fort.22) is only used to specify a few configuration parameters, while the actual wind fields are recorded in files named: `)
- NWS=13은 OWI NetCDF 입력을 선택한다. (`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=10]` ` Oceanweather Inc. (OWI) NetCDF Meteorological Input Files `; `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst line=214]` ` -  *NWS13File*: optional file name for netCDF file ( *fort.22.nc* default ) `)
- cold start와 hot start의 NWS 변경으로 hot restart가 손상되는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- Chezy·Manning·quadratic 마찰 속성은 NOLIBF가 1 또는 3이어야 한다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1850]` ` IF ((LoadChezy.or.LoadManningsN.or.LoadQuadraticFric).and. `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1851]` ` &     NoLiBF.ne.1.AND.NOLIBF.NE.3) THEN `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1855]` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `)
- 코드의 주석과 오류 문장은 NOLIBF=1만 적는다. (`[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1848]` ` C     ERROR CHECK: If Chezy, Manning's or Quadratic friction was loaded `; `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1849]` ` C     from the nodal attributes file, NOLIBF must be 1. `)

## Working Rules

1. Treat `fort.14` + `fort.15` as the canonical pair — never touch the mesh without keeping the matching `fort.15` (or you get mismatched `NSTAE`/`NSTAV` arrays).
2. Always inspect `fort.80` after a parallel run before opening `fort.61/62/...` — those files are split per PE before merge.
3. 2차원 ASCII 시계열의 첫 줄은 RUNDES,RUNID,AGRID이다. (`[file=src/globalio.F line=971]` ` write(lun, 1000) rundes, runid, agrid `) 조화분석 ASCII는 NFREQ로 시작한다. (`[file=docs/technical_reference/output_files/fort51.rst line=13]` `` :ref:`NFREQ <NFREQ>` ``; `[file=src/write_output.F line=2765]` ` write(lun,*) nfreq+nf `) binary 핫스타트는 FileFmtVersion으로 시작한다. (`[file=src/write_output.F line=5012]` ` WRITE(hss % lun,REC=IHOTSTP) FileFmtVersion `) netCDF는 해당 포맷과 변수 스키마로 판독한다. (`[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3945]` ` case (3, 367, 368) `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3946]` ` myFile%ncformat = NF90_CLOBBER `)
4. POST51은 첫 지역 파일의 NFREQ로 다른 지역 파일을 읽는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1315]` ` READ(NFREQMSG,*) NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1326]` ` DO I=1, NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1327]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),'(A80)') INLINE `) 다른 지역 파일의 주파수 수는 IDUM으로 읽는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1325]` ` READ(INLINE,*) IDUM `) 이 구간은 주파수 수의 동일성을 검사하지 않는다. (`[file=prep/post.F line=1325]` ` READ(INLINE,*) IDUM `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1326]` ` DO I=1, NFREQ `; `[file=prep/post.F line=1327]` ` READ(LOC2(IPROC),'(A80)') INLINE `) 조용히 주파수를 버리는 결과는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->

## Common Pitfalls

- 형식은 출력 제어값의 절댓값으로 확인한다. (`[file=src/read_input.F line=3627]` ` SELECT CASE(ABS(NOUTE)) `) 핫스타트 뒤 기존 파일 처리 방식은 부호로 확인한다. (`[file=src/hstart.F line=1562]` ` IF(NOUTE.LT.0) THEN `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3888]` ` if ((IHOT == 0) .or. (descript%specifier < 0) .or. & `; `[file=src/netcdfio.F90 line=3889]` ` (myFile%fileFound .eqv. .false.)) then `) 부호 변경이 부분 파일을 손상시키는지는 확인하지 않음. <!-- source-needed: 실행 결과 근거 없음 -->
- **fort.13 silent ignore** — if `NWP=0` in fort.15, the file is never read no matter what you put in it. Check `[file=src/nodalattr.F line=1051]` for `NWP` parsing.
- NWS=13의 reader는 owiwind_netcdf.F의 OWI NetCDF 경로이다. (`[file=src/owiwind_netcdf.F line=417]` ` SUBROUTINE NWS13GET(TimeLoc,WVNX2,WVNY2,PRN2, `) NWS=14의 reader는 wind.F의 NWS14INIT·NWS14GET이다. (`[file=src/wind.F line=2002]` ` CALL NWS14INIT(NWS) `; `[file=src/wind.F line=8394]` ` SUBROUTINE NWS14GET(WVNX2,WVNY2,PRN2,CICE2) `)

| 항목 | 문서·코드 대조 | 출처와 원문 | 결론 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| <a id="cvb-l197"></a> NC13-02 NWS=±13의 입력 종류 설명 | NWS 종합 페이지는 NWS=5 형식의 fort.22와 WRAMP를 설명한다. 전용 nws13.rst와 코드는 OWI netCDF 그룹 입력을 사용한다. 코드 판은 v56.2.1이다. 이 불일치는 현행 rst 사이에도 있다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=93]` ` * - 13, -13 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=94]` ` - Ramped meteorological forcing `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=95]` ` - Similar to NWS=5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs over a user-specified time period `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=96]` ` - fort.22 `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=308]` ` **NWS = 13, -13** `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws_parameters.rst line=309]` ` Similar to NWS = 5/-5, but applies a ramping function to the meteorological inputs. (OWI) NetCDF Meteorological Input Files`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/user_guide/model_configuration/meteorological_forcing/nws13.rst:12` `Motivation`<br>종합 rst는 NWS=5형 ramp 입력으로 설명한다. 전용 nws13 문서는 OWI netCDF를 설명한다. 코드도 group_order를 읽는 OWI netCDF reader를 호출한다. 해석: 종합 페이지가 다른 forcing 설명을 붙인 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=±13은 OWI netCDF 그룹 입력을 사용한다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 NC13-02](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |

| 항목 | 문서·코드 대조 | 출처와 원문 | 결론 |

| <a id="cvb-l210"></a> MET-02 NWS=2: WTIMINC 레코드 | rst 형식은 fort.22에 WTIMINC 레코드를 적는다. 문서의 설명 문장은 fort.15에 WTIMINC를 지정하도록 적는다. 코드는 fort.22에서 WTIMINC를 읽지 않는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=119]` `` :ref:`WTIMINC <WTIMINC>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=124]` ` Repeat the block above for each time step until the end of the simulation. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=133]` ` * Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1816]` ` OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1817]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1818]` ` READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP) `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:119` `` :ref:`WTIMINC <WTIMINC>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:133` `* Additional sets of met. data must be provided every WTIMINC, where WTIMINC is the met. data time interval and is specified in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1816` `OPEN(22,FILE=TRIM(INPUTDIR)//'/'//'fort.22')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1817` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX1(I),WVNY1(I),PRN1(I),I=1,NP)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1818` `READ(22,*) (NHG,WVNX2(I),WVNY2(I),PRN2(I),I=1,NP)`<br>형식표는 WTIMINC를 fort.22에 넣는다. 같은 문서의 설명은 fort.15에 지정한다고 적는다. 코드도 fort.22의 첫 줄부터 노드 자료를 읽는다. 해석: 레코드 위치의 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=2 fort.22 입력은 WTIMINC 레코드를 포함하지 않는다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-02](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l211"></a> MET-05 NWS=3의 좌표계 의무 | 문서는 ADCIRC 격자가 lon·lat이어야 한다고 적는다. 코드는 ICS=1에서 Cartesian X·Y를 사용한 별도 보간 경로도 갖는다. 이 차이는 입력 허용 경로의 차이다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=170]` ` * Meteorological data is input to a longitude, latitude grid and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be in lon, lat coordinates. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3326]` ` IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3327]` ` YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3328]` ` XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3330]` ` IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3331]` ` YCOOR=Y(I) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3332]` ` XCOOR=X(I) `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:170` `* Meteorological data is input to a longitude, latitude grid and interpolated in space onto the ADCIRC grid. The ADCIRC grid must be in lon, lat coordinates.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3326` `IF(ICS.NE.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3327` `YCOOR=SFEA(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3328` `XCOOR=SLAM(I)*RAD2DEG`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3330` `IF(ICS.EQ.1) THEN`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3331` `YCOOR=Y(I)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3332` `XCOOR=X(I)`<br>동봉 문서는 ADCIRC 격자를 lon·lat로 한정한다. reader는 ICS=1에서 X·Y를 사용한다. 해석: 격자 좌표계의 허용 경로를 잘못 한정한 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=3 보간 경로는 ICS=1의 X·Y 좌표를 처리한다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-05](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l212"></a> MET-07 NWS=±4: PRN의 단위 | 문서는 hundredths of a millibar를 적는다. 코드 주석은 MILLIBARS를 적는다. 코드는 입력값에 100을 곱한 뒤 RHOWATG로 나눈다. 해석: 이 변환이 요구하는 입력은 mbar다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=217]` ` * Wind velocity (assumed to be 10m 10 minute averaged value) must be input in knots and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in hundredths of a millibar. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=224]` ` * The following relationship is used in ADCIRC to convert to pressure in meters of water from pressure in hundredths of a millibar `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=225]` ` * PRESSURE{m H2O}=PRESSURE{Pa/100}*100/(GRAVITY*DENSITY H2O). `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3411]` ` PRN2(NHG)=100.d0*PRN2(NHG)/RHOWATG !CONVERT MILLIBARS TO M OF WATER `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3412]` ` IF(PRN2(NHG).EQ.0.) PRN2(NHG)=101300.d0/RHOWATG                       !RAL0315+ OK `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:217` `* Wind velocity (assumed to be 10m 10 minute averaged value) must be input in knots and surface atmospheric pressure must be input in hundredths of a millibar.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:224` `* The following relationship is used in ADCIRC to convert to pressure in meters of water from pressure in hundredths of a millibar`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:225` `* PRESSURE{m H2O}=PRESSURE{Pa/100}*100/(GRAVITY*DENSITY H2O).`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3411` `PRN2(NHG)=100.d0*PRN2(NHG)/RHOWATG !CONVERT MILLIBARS TO M OF WATER`<br>문서의 hundredths of a millibar와 같은 문서 식의 Pa/100 표기가 서로 다르다. 코드 주석과 100배 변환은 mbar를 가리킨다. 해석: 1 mbar=100 Pa이므로 h=100·p_mbar/(rho·g)이다. 0.01 mbar 입력이라면 h=p/(rho·g)여야 한다. 문서의 단위명이 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=±4 입력 기압 단위는 mbar이다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-07](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l213"></a> MET-09 NWS=6: 날짜·시각 여섯 필드 | 코드는 첫 레코드부터 U·V·P를 읽는다. fort.22에서 IMDAY·IMMONTH·IMYEAR·IMHOUR·IMMIN·IMSEC를 읽지 않는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=274]` ` IMDAY, IMMONTH, IMYEAR, IMHOUR, IMMIN, IMSEC `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3520]` ` DO I=1,NWLAT `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3521]` ` DO J=1,NWLON `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3522]` ` READ(22,*) WVXFN(I,J),WVYFN(I,J),PRN(I,J) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3523]` ` END DO `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3524]` ` END DO `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:274` `IMDAY, IMMONTH, IMYEAR, IMHOUR, IMMIN, IMSEC`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3520` `DO I=1,NWLAT`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3521` `DO J=1,NWLON`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3522` `READ(22,*) WVXFN(I,J),WVYFN(I,J),PRN(I,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3523` `END DO`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3524` `END DO`<br>동봉 형식표는 날짜 여섯 필드를 추가한다. 코드가 해당 레코드를 건너뛰거나 날짜로 읽지 않고 첫 줄부터 U·V·P로 읽는다. 해석: NWS=6 레코드 목록의 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=6 fort.22 입력은 첫 레코드부터 U·V·P 값을 읽는다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-09](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l214"></a> MET-41 NWS=±7의 WVXFN·WVYFN 단위 | fort22.rst 제목은 바람 응력을 적는다. 공통 매개변수 정의는 NWS=±7의 WVXFN·WVYFN을 m/s 풍속으로 적는다. 코드는 값을 응력으로 직접 사용한다. 해석: 두 필드는 물 기준 밀도로 나눈 응력 단위를 요구한다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=310]` ` NWS = 7 or -7: Wind Stress and Pressure on Rectangular Grid `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=322]` `` :ref:`WVXFN(k,j) <WVXFN>`, :ref:`WVYFN(k,j) <WVYFN>`, :ref:`PRN(k,j) <PRN>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2002]` ` (If NWS = 6, 106, 7, -7) applied horizontal wind velocity in the x,y directions. An oceanographic convention is used where velocity is positive when it is blowing toward positive coordinate directions. Units are assumed to be m/s `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3652]` ` READ(22,*) WVXFN(K,J),WVYFN(K,J),PRN(K,J) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1155]` ` WindX = WVNX1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNX2(I)-WVNX1(I)) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1156]` ` WindY = WVNY1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNY2(I)-WVNY1(I)) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1157]` ` WSX2(I) = RampMete*WindX !apply ramp `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=1158]` ` WSY2(I) = RampMete*WindY `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3691]` ` PRN2(I) = PRN2(I)/RHOWATG `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:310` `NWS = 7 or -7: Wind Stress and Pressure on Rectangular Grid`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:2002` `(If NWS = 6, 106, 7, -7) applied horizontal wind velocity in the x,y directions. An oceanographic convention is used where velocity is positive when it is blowing toward positive coordinate directions. Units are assumed to be m/s`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3652` `READ(22,*) WVXFN(K,J),WVYFN(K,J),PRN(K,J)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1155` `WindX = WVNX1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNX2(I)-WVNX1(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1156` `WindY = WVNY1(I) + WTRatio*(WVNY2(I)-WVNY1(I))`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1157` `WSX2(I) = RampMete*WindX !apply ramp`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:1158` `WSY2(I) = RampMete*WindY`<br>같은 판 fort22 제목은 응력을 적는다. 공통 정의는 m/s 풍속을 적는다. 계산은 항력 변환 없이 값을 WSX2·WSY2에 직접 넣는다. 해석: 공통 정의의 풍속 단위가 오기이다. 입력의 물리량은 물 밀도로 나눈 응력으로 차원 L²/T²이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=±7 입력 두 수평 성분은 물 밀도로 나눈 바람 응력이다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-41](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l215"></a> MET-47 NWS=±4의 prep 입력: list-directed | 문서는 각 자료 행에 I8·3E13.5 형식을 의무로 적는다. 모델 reader는 고정 형식으로 읽는다. prep reader는 같은 문자열을 list-directed로 읽는다. 해석: 고정 폭과 구분자로 나눈 형식의 허용 입력이 다르다. 실행 결과는 확인하지 않음. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=214]` ` * Each data line must have the format I8, 3E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=215]` ` * A line containing the # symbol in column 2 indicates met data at the next time increment begins on the following line. `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3396]` ` CASE(4) `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3406]` ` READ(22,'(A80)',END=9999) PBLJAGF `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3407]` ` IF(PBLJAGF(2:2).EQ.'#') THEN `<br>`[file=prep/prep.F line=3416]` ` READ(PBLJAGF,*,END=9999) NHG,U,V,PR `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3400]` ` 171     READ(PBLJAGF,'(I8,5E13.5)') NHG,WVNX2(NHG),WVNY2(NHG), `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=3401]` ` &                               PRN2(NHG) `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:214` `* Each data line must have the format I8, 3E13.5. Data input lines are repeated for as many nodes as desired.`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/prep/prep.F:3416` `READ(PBLJAGF,*,END=9999) NHG,U,V,PR`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3400` `171     READ(PBLJAGF,'(I8,5E13.5)') NHG,WVNX2(NHG),WVNY2(NHG),`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:3401` `&                               PRN2(NHG)`<br>동봉 문서는 I8·3E13.5를 요구한다. 모델 reader는 고정 형식으로 읽는다. prep는 같은 레코드를 list-directed로 읽는다. 해석: 동일 문서와 다른 reader 경로에 모순되는 입력 해석의 결함 후보이다. 모든 정상 파일에서 실패한다고 확대하지 않는다. | E 코드 결함 후보. v56.2.1의 NWS=±4 prep reader가 고정 폭 레코드를 list-directed로 해석하는 동작은 결함 후보이다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-47](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| MET-32 NWS=15·16: 입력 디렉터리 | -I의 일반 입력 디렉터리 설명과 달리 이 두 초기화 루틴은 globaldir에서 fort.22를 연다. CLI-01의 예외이다. | `[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=11]` ``` ``-I INPUTDIR`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/running_adcirc/command_line_options.rst line=12]` ` Look for input files in the directory 'INPUTDIR' instead of looking in the directory where the ADCIRC command was issued. `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6258]` ` call openFileForRead(22,trim(globaldir)//"/fort.22",errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6971]` ` call openFileForRead(22,trim(globaldir)//"/fort.22",errorio) `<br>[근거·판정 정본](#cvb-l199) | [근거·판정 정본](#cvb-l199) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l217"></a> MET-34 NWS=15: HWind 격자 간격 | 문서는 입력 격자 간격을 m로 적는다. 코드는 km로 읽고 1000을 곱한다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=496]` ` * the mercator grid spacing is in meters and is uniform in x and y (dx=dy) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6723]` ` read(220,'(7X,F8.5,A)',end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6724]` ` &   hWindFiles(cn)%dh `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6725]` ` write(scratchMessage,'("Mercator grid spacing is ",F8.5," km.")') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=6787]` ` hWindFiles(cn)%dh = hWindFiles(cn)%dh * 1000.d0 `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:496` `* the mercator grid spacing is in meters and is uniform in x and y (dx=dy)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6723` `read(220,'(7X,F8.5,A)',end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6724` `&   hWindFiles(cn)%dh`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6725` `write(scratchMessage,'("Mercator grid spacing is ",F8.5," km.")')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6726` `&    hWindFiles(cn)%dh`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6787` `hWindFiles(cn)%dh = hWindFiles(cn)%dh * 1000.d0`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:6786` `! convert mercator mesh spacing dh from km to m`<br>문서는 입력 간격을 m로 적는다. 코드의 로그·변환 주석·1000배 식은 km 입력을 가리킨다. 해석: 입력 단위와 변환 후 단위를 혼동한 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1은 HWind 격자 간격을 km로 읽어 m로 바꾼다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-34](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l218"></a> MET-38 NWS=16: 예제의 Pc 열 | 예제는 시간·Pc·ramp·파일 이름 네 필드를 적는다. 형식 정의와 코드는 시간·ramp·파일 이름 세 필드를 사용한다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=535]` `` :ref:`hours(i) <hours>` :ref:`rampMult(i) <rampMult>` :ref:`filename(i) <filename>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=569]` ` 0.0 -1 0.0 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1330" ! time (hours), Pc (mb), ramp mult, filename `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=570]` ` 6.0 -1 0.5 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1930" `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7009]` ` read(22,*,end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7010]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%cycleTime, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7011]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%rampVal, `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7012]` ` &     GFDL_Files(i)%file_name `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:535` `` :ref:`hours(i) <hours>` :ref:`rampMult(i) <rampMult>` :ref:`filename(i) <filename>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:569` `0.0 -1 0.0 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1330" ! time (hours), Pc (mb), ramp mult, filename`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:570` `6.0 -1 0.5 "/home/jason/hwind/al092011_0828_1930"`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7009` `read(22,*,end=246,err=248,iostat=errorio)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7010` `&     GFDL_Files(i)%cycleTime,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7011` `&     GFDL_Files(i)%rampVal,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7012` `&     GFDL_Files(i)%file_name`<br>예제는 Pc가 있는 네 필드를 적는다. 같은 문서의 형식 정의와 실제 READ는 세 필드다. 해석: 다른 형식의 예제를 남긴 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=16 제어 레코드는 시간·ramp·파일 이름을 읽는다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-38](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l219"></a> MET-42 NWS=16: NCELLS의 형식 | 문서는 F10.4를 적는다. 현재 코드는 I10을 사용한다. F10.4를 읽는 옛 줄은 주석이다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst line=545]` ` * Line 1: Number of grid cells (f10.4) NCELLS `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7251]` ` !       READ(LUN,'(F10.4)') RPTS_GFDL `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7252]` ` !       NPTS_GFDL = INT(RPTS_GFDL) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=7253]` ` read(LUN,'(I10)') NPTS_GFDL `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:545` `* Line 1: Number of grid cells (f10.4) NCELLS`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7251` `!       READ(LUN,'(F10.4)') RPTS_GFDL`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7252` `!       NPTS_GFDL = INT(RPTS_GFDL)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7253` `read(LUN,'(I10)') NPTS_GFDL`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort22.rst:556` `* column 10: nest number (this is not always present)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7264` `!          READ(LUN,'(10F10.4)') UTMP,VTMP,TTMP,MTMP,SPTMP,PTMP,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7265` `!     &                      LONGTMP,LATTMP,HTMP,NTMP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7266` `READ(LUN,'(9F10.4)') UTMP,VTMP,TTMP,MTMP,SPTMP,PTMP,`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7267` `&                      LONGTMP,LATTMP,HTMP`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:7256` `!       tcm 20130507 -- changed the read above due to changes in GFDL ASCII files`<br>옛 F10.4와 열 10 READ는 주석으로 남아 있다. 20130507 주석은 GFDL ASCII 변경에 따른 reader 수정을 명시한다. 해석: 문서는 변경 전 형식을 남겼다. 현재는 I10과 아홉 실수 필드를 읽는다. 선택 열 10은 있더라도 읽지 않는다. | A 판 차이. v56.2.1의 NWS=16 reader는 NCELLS를 I10으로 읽고 각 지점의 첫 아홉 필드만 사용한다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 MET-42, MET-44](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l220"></a> F200-05 NWS=11: UE·VE·PE의 저장 순서 | 문서는 각 j에 UE(j)·VE(j)·PE(j)를 나란히 적는다. 코드는 한 unformatted 레코드에서 UE 전체·VE 전체·PE 전체 순서로 읽는다. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=27]` `` for j=1, :ref:`LONB <LONB>` * :ref:`LATB <LATB>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=28]` `` :ref:`UE(j) <UE>`, :ref:`VE(j) <VE>`, :ref:`PE(j) <PE>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=29]` ` end j loop `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4196]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4201]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:27` `` for j=1, :ref:`LONB <LONB>` * :ref:`LATB <LATB>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:28` `` :ref:`UE(j) <UE>`, :ref:`VE(j) <VE>`, :ref:`PE(j) <PE>` ``<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:29` `end j loop`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4196` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4201` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE`<br>동봉 문서는 j마다 UE·VE·PE를 교차 나열한다. 실제 unformatted READ는 UE 전체·VE 전체·PE 전체 순서다. 해석: binary 배열의 저장 순서를 잘못 표시한 문서 오기이다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 NWS=11 reader는 한 레코드에서 UE·VE·PE 전체 배열을 차례로 읽는다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 F200-05](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l221"></a> F200-06 NWS=11: binary OPEN의 FORM | 문서는 FORM='unformatted'를 요구한다. 실제 호출한 openFileForRead는 FORM을 지정하지 않는다. 해석: 기본 formatted 연결과 unformatted READ가 충돌한다. 실행 결과는 확인하지 않음. | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst line=81]` ` - File type: binary (ACCESS='sequential', FORM='unformatted') `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4187]` ` call openFileForRead(IEXT,FNAME1,errorIO) `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4195]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) IYEAR,IMONTH,IDAY,IHOUR `<br>`[file=src/wind.F line=4196]` ` READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE `<br>`[file=src/io.F90 line=100]` ` open (lun, file=trim(filename), status='OLD', & `<br>`[file=src/io.F90 line=101]` ` action='READ', iostat=errorIO) `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort200.rst:81` `- File type: binary (ACCESS='sequential', FORM='unformatted')`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4187` `call openFileForRead(IEXT,FNAME1,errorIO)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4195` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) IYEAR,IMONTH,IDAY,IHOUR`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4196` `READ(IEXT,END=1100) UE,VE,PE`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/io.F90:100` `open (lun, file=trim(filename), status='OLD', &`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/io.F90:101` `action='READ', iostat=errorIO)`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4113` `C   The input files are in binary and have been created by NOAA and    *`<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/source_code/adcirc/src/wind.F:4114` `C   contain only the U 10M, V 10M, (M/S) and surface P fields (mbars). *`<br>동봉 문서와 자체 주석은 binary 입력을 정의한다. 실제 호출한 openFileForRead는 FORM을 생략한다. 이어지는 READ는 unformatted 문이다. 해석: 같은 판 문서·자체 주석·다른 I/O 경로의 형식에 모순되는 연결 형식의 결함 후보이다. | E 코드 결함 후보. v56.2.1의 NWS=11 binary reader가 formatted 기본 OPEN을 사용하는 동작은 결함 후보이다.<br>[inputfiles 보고서 §5 F200-06](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-inputfiles-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| <a id="cvb-l222"></a> F200-07 NWS=11: 기압 단위 주장 | 문서는 입력 기압을 millibar로 적는다. 문서의 식과 코드는 기압을 100*RHOWAT0*G로 나눈다. 해석: millibar를 Pa로 바꾸는 100배 변환과 반대이다. ## 문서와 코드(v56.2.1) 입력이 다른 곳

문서 판과 코드 판이 달라 생기는 차이가 섞여 있다. 문서에는 현행 RST·위키·웹사이트 v53·PDF v44.XX·1990년대 보고서가 포함된다. 코드는 v56.2.1이다. 아래 행의 최종 결론은 두 입력 대조 보고서 §5를 따른다. PDF를 직접 근거로 사용한 입력 판정은 이 표에 없다. Each digit represents a specific option regarding the dimension and `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:78` ` the formulation of certain terms or integration methods in the GWCE or momentum `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:79` ` equations. The available options for each digit are specified below, with the `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/im.rst:80` ` first digit being the left-most. The internal flags that are set are listed to `<br>`src/read_input.F:1233` ` ELSEIF ((IM.GE.111111).AND.(IM.LE.734323)) THEN `<br>`src/read_input.F:1286` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1297` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1323` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1337` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1347` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1370` ` ENDIF `<br>`src/read_input.F:1377` ` ELSE `<br>`src/read_input.F:1378` ` CALL terminate(exit_code=ADCIRC_EXIT_FAILURE, `<br>해석: 문서는 각 자리의 사용 가능한 선택을 제시하지만 코드는 전체 정수 범위를 먼저 검사한 뒤 각 IF 블록을 종료한다. 문서 밖 자리 값을 별도로 terminate하는 기본 분기가 없다. 예를 들어 범위 안의 IM=120111이 전체 범위를 통과해도 0인 세 번째 자리의 의미까지 검증했다고 볼 수 없다. | 문서의 20/30과 현재 종료 처리가 다르다. 자리별 문서도 첫 자리 7과 마지막 자리의 추가 선택을 빠뜨린다. | A 판 차이. v56.2.1은 IM=20과 IM=30을 종료시키고 2DDI 밀도 강제를 densityControl로 지정하게 한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L67, L644, L646](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>C 문서 누락. 허용 범위 안의 여섯 자리 IM은 첫 자리 6 또는 7에서 C3D와 C3DVS를 켠다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L654, C26 (L864)](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>C 문서 누락. 허용 범위 안의 여섯 자리 IM은 마지막 자리 0에서 Ali 보정을 켜고 3보다 큰 값에서 CBaroclinic을 켠다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L672, L673](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>C 문서 누락. IM의 전체 정수 허용 검사 통과는 각 자리 조합의 유효성 보장을 뜻하지 않는다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L674](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |
| fort.15 / ` NOLIBF ` | 2DDI 마찰 0=선형,1=제곱,2=혼합. 3D에서는 무시. Chezy/Manning/제곱 마찰 속성에는 반드시 1이며 다른 값이면 종료한다고 적는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=387]` ` Parameter controlling the type of bottom stress parameterization used in a 2DDI ADCIRC run. This parameter must be specified but is ignored in a 3D run. Note: In the NWP section, if the user selects quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=389]` ` = 0 linear bottom friction law. The friction coefficient (FFACTOR) is specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=391]` ` = 1 quadratic bottom friction law. The friction coefficient (FFACTOR) is specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=393]` ` = 2 hybrid nonlinear bottom friction law. In deep water, the friction coefficient is constant and a quadratic bottom friction law results. In shallow water the friction coefficient increases as the depth decreases (e.g. as in a Manning-type friction law). The friction coefficient is determined as: FFACTOR=FFACTORMIN*[1+(HBREAK/H)**FTHETA]**(FGAMMA/FTHETA). The required parameters (FFACTORMIN, HBREAK, FTHETA, FGAMMA) are specified below. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=395]` ` For 3D ADCIRC runs, spatially varying bottom friction should be specified using the bottom_roughness_length nodal attribute. `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=47]` `` In the :ref:`NWP <nwp>` section, if the user selects ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=48]` ` quadratic_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, mannings_n_at_sea_floor, or `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=49]` ` chezy_friction_coefficient_at_sea_floor, then NOLIBF must be 1 (nonlinear `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=50]` ` friction formulation) since all those formulations are nonlinear. If the NOLIBF `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/nolibf.rst line=51]` ` were anything other than 1, it is an error that will cause ADCIRC to stop. ` | 0..3을 허용한다. 3은 | fort.15 / ` CORI ` | 공간 일정 Coriolis 계수.1/s. 정의는 항상 읽고 NCOR=0에서만 사용한다고 적는다. RST 형식은 NCOR=1을 조건으로 적는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=754]` ` Constant Coriolis coefficient. This value is always read in, however it is only used in the computations when NCOR = 0. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst line=61]` `` :ref:`CORI <CORI>`      if NCOR = 1 ``<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/wiki/markdown/Fort.15_file_format.md line=81]` `` `[CORI](/index.php?title=CORI&action=edit&redlink=1)` - constant Coriolis coefficient, only used if `NCOR=0` ``<br>`[file=models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/input-file-descriptions/model-parameter-and-periodic-boundary-condition-file-fort-15.md line=502]` ` [**CORI**](https://adcirc.org/home/documentation/users-manual-v53/parameter-definitions#CORI) ` | 항상 읽는다. NCOR=0이면 CORIF=CORI.1이면 위도로 계산한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3315]` ` READ(15,*) CORI `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3317]` ` DO I=1,NP `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3318]` ` CORIF(I)=CORI `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=3326]` ` CORIF(I)=2.0d0*omega*SIN(SFEA(I)) `<br>확인한 원문: `docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst:61` `` :ref:`CORI <CORI>`      if NCOR = 1 ``<br>`docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:754` ` Constant Coriolis coefficient. This value is always read in, however it is only used in the computations when NCOR = 0. `<br>`src/read_input.F:3315` ` READ(15,*) CORI `<br>`src/read_input.F:3316` ` IF(NCOR.EQ.0) THEN `<br>`src/read_input.F:3318` ` CORIF(I)=CORI `<br>해석: 현행 형식 문서만 NCOR=1 조건을 붙인다. 같은 판 일반 정의의 always read 및 NCOR=0 사용과 코드가 일치한다. | RST 형식의 조건이 반대이다. 정의·위키·v53 형식은 일치한다. v53 문서와 v56.2.1 코드의 판 차이만으로 설명되지 않는 현행 RST 오류이다. | B 문서 오기. CORI 줄은 항상 읽으며 상수 Coriolis 계산은 NCOR=0에서 사용한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L106](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |
| fort.15 / ` TAU0 ` | GWCE 가중과 음수 옵션. 양수 상수, -1/-2/-3/-5/-6/-7, 소수 첫 자리 1의 출력을 설명한다. 인용한 정의 값표는 단위를 명시하지 않는다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=497]` ` Generalized Wave-Continuity Equation (GWCE) weighting factor that weights the relative contribution of the primitive and wave portions of the GWCE. If "primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation" is specified as a nodal attribute in the fort.15 file above, this line will be read in but ignored. If a nodal attribute file is not used or "primitive_weighting_in_continuity_equation" is in the nodal attribute (fort.13) file, but not specified in the fort.15 file this TAU0 parameter will be used. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=499]` ` = 0 the GWCE is a pure wave equation. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=501]` ` < 1 the GWCE behaves like a pure primitive continuity equation. A good rule of thumb for setting TAU0 is to set it equal to the largest value of an equivalent linear friction factor (e.g, for linear friction TAU0 = TAU; for quadratic friction TAU0 = maximum (speed*CF/depth). Typical values for TAU0 are in the range of 0.005 – 0.1. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=503]` ` = -1 the TAU0 is spatially varying but constant in time; it is calculated according to depth as follows: If the depth is >=10 TAU0 is set to 0.005, if the depth is < 10, TAU0 is set to 0.020. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=505]` ` = -2 the TAU0 is spatially varying but constant in time; it is calculated according to depth as follows: if the depth is >=200 TAU0 is set to 0.005, if the depth is < 200 but > 1, then TAU0 is set to 1/depth, and if depth < 1, TAU0 is set to 1.0. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=507]` ` = -3 the TAU0 varies spatially and in time. TAU0 is computed from TAU0Base read in from nodal attribute file. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=509]` ` if TAU0Base < 0.025; TAU0 = TAU0Base (constant in time) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=511]` ` if TAU0Base >= 0.025; TAU0 = TAU0Base + 1.5 TK(i) where TK(i)=Cd\\|U\\|/H `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=521]` ` = -5, FullDomainTimeVaryingTau0 = .True. the TAU0 varies spatially and in time, and is dependent on the local friction; it is limited to a range specified by Tau0FullDomainMin and Tau0FullDomainMax. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=525]` ` For tau0 formulations that vary spatially and temporally, ADCIRC is capable of writing out the tau0 values that it calculates internally. These values are written to a fort.90 file, which has the same format and output frequency as the water surface elevation output file (fort.63). The production of a fort.90 file is specified by placing a 1 in the tenths place of the tau0 input valuie in the fort.15 file. For example, if tau0=-3.1, the calculation of tau0 is still carried out according to the description of tau0=-3 above, and the fort.90 output file will also be produced. `<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=169]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -3, -6, or -7``, :math:`\tau_0` varies in time and based on ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=182]` `` A positive value is ignored if the :ref:`primitive weighting in continuity ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=188]` `` GWCE is a pure wave equation for :math:`\tau_0=0`, and approaches a pure ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=189]` `` primitive equation as :math:`\tau_0\rightarrow\infty`, however in practice, it ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=190]` `` behaves like a pure primitive equation once :math:`\tau_0` reaches 1 or so. ``<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=204]` ``` -  ``If depth >= 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=205]` ``` -  ``If depth < 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.020`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=212]` ``` -  ``If depth >= 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=213]` ``` -  ``If 1 < depth < 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 1/depth`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=214]` ``` -  ``If depth < 1; then``\ :math:`\tau_0 = 1` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=227]` ``` -  ``If TAU0Base < 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base`` (constant in ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=229]` ``` -  ``If TAU0Base >= 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=245]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -6``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``, ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=248]` ``` -  ``TAU0 = -7``, :math:`\tau_0` is defined using the rules for ``TAU0 = -3``, ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=252]` ``` -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR + (1.0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0``, where ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=254]` ``` ``AlphaTau0 = 0.25`` is hard-coded into the model. This means that ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=313]` ``` output is activated by placing a 1 in the tenths place of ``TAU0`` in the ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=314]` ``` fort.15 file. For example, if ``TAU0 = -3.1``, the calculation of :math:`\tau_0` ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=315]` ``` is still carried out according to the description of ``TAU0 = -3`` above, and ```<br>`[file=docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst line=316]` ` the fort.90 output file will also be produced. ` | 항상 읽는다. -5 계열은 min/max 줄을 읽는다. 절점 속성 초기화가 음수 자리·시간 변화·출력·필수 속성을 처리한다. 초기화의 `IF (.not.LoadTau0) THEN` 안의 `IF (Tau0.ge.0) THEN`은 상수 Tau0의 단위를 1/sec로 기록한다(`src/nodalattr.F:1909` `IF (.not.LoadTau0) THEN`, `src/nodalattr.F:1910` `IF (Tau0.ge.0) THEN`, `src/nodalattr.F:1914` `WRITE(16,7) Tau0`, `src/nodalattr.F:1919` `&           /,5X,'Tau0 = ',E15.8,2X,'1/sec',/)`). Suggested values are Tau0FullDomainMin = 0.005 and Tau0FullDomainMax = 0.2. `<br>`src/read_input.F:2505` ` IF ( (Tau0.le.-5.d0).and.(Tau0.gt.-6.d0) ) THEN `<br>`src/read_input.F:2506` ` READ(15,*) Tau0FullDomainMin, Tau0FullDomainMax `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1979` ` IF ( .not.LoadTau0MinMax ) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1981` ` Tau0MinMax(I,1) = Tau0FullDomainMin `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1982` ` Tau0MinMax(I,2) = Tau0FullDomainMax `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2190` ` Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0MinMax(i,1)+1.5*TK(i) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2194` ` IF(Tau0Temp(i).gt.Tau0MinMax(i,2)) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2195` ` Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0MinMax(i,2) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2213` ` CaseySum = CaseySum + Tau0Temp(NeiTab(I,J)) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2215` ` TAU0VAR(I) = CaseySum / NNeigh(I) `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:270` `` applied during the update. Each node's :math:`\tau_0` is then spatially averaged ``<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:271` ``` with all immediate neighbors. Time-averaging (for ``TAU0 = -6 or -7``) is ```<br>해석: 문서의 정확히 -5 조건은 같은 문서의 -x.1 출력 선택과 양립하지 않는다. 코드는 -6<TAU0<=-5에서 min/max 줄을 반드시 읽는다. 문서의 suggested values는 생략 기본값이 아니다. 절점 제한 후 이웃 평균을 적용하므로 서로 다른 절점 경계 안에 최종값이 남는다는 보장은 없다.<br>확인한 원문: `docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:204` ``` -  ``If depth >= 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:205` ``` -  ``If depth < 10; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.020`` ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:212` ``` -  ``If depth >= 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 0.005`` ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:213` ``` -  ``If 1 < depth < 200; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= 1/depth`` ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:214` ``` -  ``If depth < 1; then``\ :math:`\tau_0 = 1` ```<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2149` ` IF (Scheme.eq.-2.d0) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2151` ` IF(Depth.GE.200.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005 `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2152` ` IF((Depth.LT.200.).AND.(Depth.GE.1.)) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2153` ` Tau0NodalValue=1./Depth `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2155` ` IF(Depth.LT.1.) Tau0NodalValue=1.0 `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2158` ` IF(Depth.LE.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.020d0 `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2159` ` IF(Depth.GT.10.) Tau0NodalValue=0.005d0 `<br>해석: 같은 판 RST는 -1의 depth=10에 0.005를 적지만 코드는 <=10에 0.020을 대입한다. -2의 문서 구간은 depth=1을 빠뜨렸고 코드는 >=1 분기로 1을 만든다. exact Scheme=-2가 아니면 두 구간 초기화를 사용하므로 -2.1을 -2와 같은 초기값 선택으로 일반화하지 않는다.<br>확인한 원문: `docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:229` ``` -  ``If TAU0Base >= 0.025; then``\ :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= TAU0Base+1.5*TK`` ```<br>`docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:511` ` if TAU0Base >= 0.025; TAU0 = TAU0Base + 1.5 TK(i) where TK(i)=Cd\\|U\\|/H `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2201` ` IF(Tau0Base(i).lt.0.025) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2202` ` Tau0Temp(I)=Tau0Base(i) ! not time varying `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2204` ` Tau0Temp(i)=Tau0Base(i)+1.5*TK(i) ! time varying `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2205` ` IF (Tau0Temp(i).gt.0.2) Tau0Temp(i)=0.2 ! ceiling `<br>`src/gwce.F:441` ` C              WJP 02.24.2018 Get the infinity norm of the matrix `<br>`src/gwce.F:442` ` TK(I) = max(abs(TKM(1,I))+abs(TKM(3,I)), `<br>`src/gwce.F:443` ` &                     abs(TKM(2,I))+abs(TKM(3,I))) `<br>`src/gwce.F:445` ` CALL CalculateTimeVaryingTau0(TK, NNeigh, NeiTab, NP) `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:252` ``` -  :math:`\tau_0`\ ``= AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR + (1.0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0``, where ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:253` ``` ``TAU0VAR`` is ``Tau0Base`` after spatial averaging, and ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:254` ``` ``AlphaTau0 = 0.25`` is hard-coded into the model. This means that ```<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2213` ` CaseySum = CaseySum + Tau0Temp(NeiTab(I,J)) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2215` ` TAU0VAR(I) = CaseySum / NNeigh(I) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2221` ` TAU0VAR(I) = 0.5d0*TAU0VAR(I) + 0.5d0*LastTau0(I) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2229` ` TAU0VAR(I) = AlphaTau0*TAU0VAR(I) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:2230` ` &                 + (1.d0-AlphaTau0)*LastTau0(I) `<br>해석: 코드의 2018-02-24 주석이 TK를 마찰 행렬의 무한대 노름으로 정함을 명시한다. 문서는 이전 스칼라 Cd·\|U\|/H를 일반식으로 유지한다. 코드는 원시 TAU0Temp에 0.2 상한도 적용하며 문서는 그 상한을 누락했다. 실제 이웃 평균 및 -6/-7 시간 평균은 그 다음이다.<br>확인한 원문: `docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:525` ` The production of a fort.90 file is specified by placing a 1 in the tenths place of the tau0 input valuie in the fort.15 file. `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1830` ` Tau0Dig1 = INT(Tau0)   ! jgf47.30 truncate the fractional part `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1831` ` !jgf47.34 round away from zero by subtracting 0.5d0 `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1832` ` Tau0Dig2 = INT( (Tau0 - REAL(Tau0Dig1))*10.0d0 - 0.5d0) `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1989` ` IF ( Tau0Dig2.eq.-1 ) THEN `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1990` ` OutputTau0 = .True. `<br>`src/nodalattr.F:1829` ` IF (Tau0.lt.0) THEN `<br>해석: 코드가 jgf47.34의 round away from zero 방식을 명시한다. 문서의 소수 첫 자리라는 설명은 잘라내기와 반올림을 구별하지 않는다. -3.1 예시는 맞지만 -3.19는 Tau0Dig1=-3, Tau0Dig2=INT(-1.9-0.5)=-2가 되어 출력 플래그를 켜지 않는다.<br>확인한 원문: `docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:262` ``` For ``TAU0 = -3, -5, -6, or -7``, :math:`\tau_0` is updated (via the ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:263` ``` ``CalculateTimeVaryingTau0`` subroutine) in space and in time. An initial ```<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:264` ` "update" is done when the model starts. After this, updates are done only after `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:265` ` a time step in which there is a change in wet/dry state somewhere in the model `<br>`docs/user_guide/model_configuration/model_parameters/tau0.rst:266` ` domain. For use cases that contain large number of nodes near the wet/dry `<br>`src/gwce.F:425` ` C..... DW, Dev 2019 `<br>`src/gwce.F:426` ` C.....  if CGWCE_HDP = .true. use H instead of DP in the surface gradient term `<br>`src/gwce.F:427` ` NCIFBLCK: IF(NCChange.GT.0 .or. CGWCE_HDP ) THEN !if any subdomain grid has changed `<br>`src/gwce.F:437` ` IF(C2DDI.AND. `<br>`src/gwce.F:438` ` &       ((FullDomainTimeVaryingTau0.or.HighResTimeVaryingTau0))) `<br>`src/gwce.F:439` ` &   THEN `<br>`src/gwce.F:445` ` CALL CalculateTimeVaryingTau0(TK, NNeigh, NeiTab, NP) `<br>해석: 코드의 Dev 2019 주석과 CGWCE_HDP 조건이 implicit finite-amplitude 경로의 갱신을 명시한다. wet/dry 변화에만 연결한 설명이 이 경로를 빠뜨린다. | 일반 정의의 'ignored'는 음수 제어까지 포괄한다. depth=10 경계가 다르다. -5.x 줄 조건과 0.2 상한을 문서가 빠뜨린다. 2D 코드의 TK는 하상 마찰 행렬의 무한대 노름이다. CGWCE_HDP도 시간 변화 갱신을 유발한다. 소수 자리 출력 검사는 반올림을 사용한다. | B 문서 오기. fort.13의 primitive_weighting 속성은 TAU0의 음수 제어 선택을 무효화하지 않는다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L80, L714](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>B 문서 오기. -6<TAU0<=-5이면 코드는 다음 줄의 min/max를 읽어 절점 제한 후 공간 평균에 사용한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L81, L82, L716, C02 (L840)](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 기본 두 구간 TAU0 초기화는 수심 10 이하에서 0.020을 사용한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L712, L713, C01 (L839)](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>A 판 차이. v56.2.1의 2D 시간 변화 TAU0 계산은 마찰 행렬의 무한대 노름을 TK로 사용한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L715](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>A 판 차이. fort.90 출력 선택은 음수 TAU0에서 계산한 Tau0Dig2가 -1일 때 켜진다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L719](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론)<br>A 판 차이. CGWCE_HDP가 참이면 시간 변화 TAU0 갱신 경로는 wet/dry 변화가 없어도 실행된다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L720](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |

| fort.15 / ` ITITER ` | -1=lumped explicit GWCE. 1=iterative JCG.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1237]` ` ITITER = Parameters that provide information about the solver that will be used for the GWCE. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1239]` ` ITITER = -1 only for lumped, explicit GWCE, matrix is diagonal and no external solver is needed `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1241]` ` ITITER = 1 use iterative JCG solver (from ITPACKV 2D) `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=1261]` ` Note: all of the parameters must be input regardless of whether a diagonal or iterative solver is selected. However, ISLDIA, CONVCR and ITMAX are only used with the iterative solvers ` | 항상 읽지만 ITITER 선택 검사는 없다. GWCE는 ILump==0이면 JCG를 호출한다. 그 외에는 lumped 계산을 한다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=4581]` ` READ(15,*) ITITER,ISLDIA,CONVCR,ITMAX `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=141]` ` IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=145]` ` CALL DFAULT(IPARM,RPARM) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2000]` ` IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2003]` ` CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS, `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2004]` ` &        IWKSP,NW,WKSP,IPARM,RPARM,IER) `<br>`[file=src/gwce.F line=2009]` ` ELSE ! lumped LHS `<br>확인한 원문: `docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:1239` ` ITITER = -1 only for lumped, explicit GWCE, matrix is diagonal and no external solver is needed `<br>`docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:1241` ` ITITER = 1 use iterative JCG solver (from ITPACKV 2D) `<br>`src/read_input.F:1349` ` IF (IMDig6.EQ.1) THEN `<br>`src/read_input.F:1350` ` ILump=0 `<br>`src/read_input.F:1352` ` ELSEIF (IMDig6.EQ.2) THEN `<br>`src/read_input.F:1353` ` ILump=1 `<br>`src/read_input.F:4581` ` READ(15,*) ITITER,ISLDIA,CONVCR,ITMAX `<br>`src/gwce.F:2000` ` IF (ILump.eq.0) THEN ! default, fully consistent LHS `<br>`src/gwce.F:2003` ` CALL JCG(NP,MNP,MNEI,NEITAB,COEF,GWCE_LV,ETAS, `<br>`src/gwce.F:2004` ` &        IWKSP,NW,WKSP,IPARM,RPARM,IER) `<br>`src/gwce.F:2009` ` ELSE ! lumped LHS `<br>해석: 현행 문서는 ITITER를 솔버 선택으로 설명하지만 GWCE는 IM으로 정한 ILump에서 JCG와 대각 경로를 나눈다. src/gwce.F와 src/read_input.F에서 ITITER를 검색하여 읽기 외 선택 분기가 없음을 확인했다. | 문서가 ITITER를 솔버 선택으로 설명한다. 실제 분기는 IM에서 정한 ILump를 사용한다. | B 문서 오기. v56.2.1의 GWCE 솔버 경로는 ITITER가 아니라 IM에서 정한 ILump로 선택한다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L240](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |

REAL(8), PARAMETER :: RhoAir = 1.15D0 !...Holland `<br>해석: 코드는 1.293을 변경 가능한 기본값으로 명시하고 1.15를 Holland의 다른 선택으로 주석에 남긴다. 현행 문서는 대체값을 기본값으로 적었다. 값의 변경 시점을 추정하지 않는다. | 초기 밀도 1.293과 문서 기본값 1.15가 다르다. | B 문서 오기. metControl.rhoAir의 코드 기본값은 1.293 kg/m³이다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L495](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |
| fort.15 / ` waveCoupling.WaveWindMultiplier ` | 변수 이름의 fort.15 입력 정의를 찾지 못했다.<br> | REAL(8) 기본 1. waveCoupling에서 읽는다. 문서 철자 WindWaveMultiplier는 이 선언에 없다. 일반 namelist 오류 처리는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=333]` ` NAMELIST /waveCoupling/ WaveWindMultiplier, `<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=593]` ` read(unit=15,nml=waveCoupling,iostat=ios,iomsg=ios_nml_error_msg) `<br>`[file=src/constants.F90 line=119]` ` real(8) :: waveWindMultiplier = 1.0d0 `<br>확인한 원문: `docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort15.rst:206` `` :ref:`&waveCoupling <waveCoupling>` :ref:`WindWaveMultiplier=floatValue <WindWaveMultiplier>` / ``<br>`docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst:2888` ` WindWaveMultiplier `<br>`src/read_input.F:333` ` NAMELIST /waveCoupling/ WaveWindMultiplier, `<br>`src/read_input.F:334` ` &         Limit_WaveStressGrad, WaveStressGrad_Cap `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v51/version-history-v51/index.md:1118` ` **v51.01 – 10/19/2012 – jcd** `<br>`models/ADCIRC/raw/manuals/website_markdown/home/documentation/users-manual-v51/version-history-v51/index.md:1120` ` Added a WaveWindMultiplier to allow the user to adjust the wind speeds before they are passed to the wave model. The conversion factor has a default value of 1.0, but it can be adjusted in the fort.15 file with a command like: &waveCoupling WaveWindMultiplier=1.09 / `<br>해석: v51.01의 변경 이력은 WaveWindMultiplier=1.09 입력 예제를 제시한다. 현재 코드도 그 철자를 사용한다. 현행 RST가 WindWaveMultiplier로 뒤집어 쓴 오기이다. [10-12 결론 정정] 805행의 문서 미정의 취급은 v51 변경 이력까지 확인한 범위에서는 성립하지 않는다. | 문서에 WindWaveMultiplier라는 다른 철자만 있다. 2절의 철자 불일치에도 포함했다. | B 문서 오기. waveCoupling의 정확한 입력 이름은 WaveWindMultiplier이다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L569, L591, L805](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) |

| <a id="cvb-l249"></a> fort.15 / ` TVWControl.NOUT_TVW ` | 출력 형식 0/±1/±3/±4/±5. 미지정이면 NOUTGE를 상속한다.<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=2989]` ` Integer that activates the production of the fort.77 output file as well as specifying its format; setting nout_TVW to zero turns off the production of the fort.77 output file while setting it to positive or negative 1, 3, 4, or 5 produce files in ASCII, netCDF3, sparse ASCII, or netCDF4, respectively. Due to the sparse nature of the weir height output data, sparse ASCII format (nout_TVW=4) is recommended. If use_TVW is set to .true. but this parameter nout_TVW is not set at all, then nout_TVW is set to the same value as the file format specifier for the fort.63 file (NOUTGE). The data stored in the fort.77 file represent the change in the weir height (in meters, positive upward) compared to the weir height specified in the ADCIRC mesh file, rather than the absolute height of the weir at a particular time. ` | 초기 sentinel -99999. USE_TVW이면 지정하지 않은 값을 NOUTGE/TOUTSGE/TOUTFGE/NSPOOLGE에서 가져온다. 이 대입은 해당 fort.15 전역 출력 줄 READ보다 앞선다. 이후 일정 계산은 STATIM을 뺀다. 읽기 오류 예외는 2.8.1에 적었다.<br>`[file=src/read_input.F line=257]` ` NAMELIST NTCYF_TVW=INT((TOUTF_TVW-STATIM)*(86400.D0/DTDP) + 0.5d0) `<br>`src/read_input.F:4285` ` NDSETS_TVW=INT((NTCYF_TVW-NTCYS_TVW)/NSPOOL_TVW) `<br>해석: 문서는 fort.63 설정을 상속한다고 설명한다. 실제 복사는 fort.63 설정을 읽는 READ보다 먼저 실행한다. 뒤의 일반 전역 출력 경로는 새 값을 쓰지만 TVW 일정 계산은 먼저 복사한 값을 사용한다. 같은 판 문서와 다른 출력 경로의 두 근거가 충돌하는 읽기 순서 결함 후보이다. src/read_input.F의 NOUT_TVW·TOUTS_TVW·TOUTF_TVW·NSPOOL_TVW 대입을 검색하여 뒤에서 재상속하는 대입이 없음을 확인했다. | 해석: 상속 대입은 NOUTGE 위치 입력을 읽기 전에 수행한다. 그 뒤 전역 출력 READ로 갱신한 값을 다시 상속하는 대입은 없다. 실제 위치 입력의 기본 일정 상속을 보장하지 않는다. | E 코드 결함 후보. TVWControl의 생략값 상속은 전역 출력 설정을 읽기 전에 수행하는 결함 후보이다.<br>[fort15 보고서 §5 L518, L519, L520, L521](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/ADCIRC-fort15-vs-code.md#5-차이-결론) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |

| F14-12 / IBTYPEE | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort14.rst line=26]` `` :ref:`NVDLL(k) <NVDLL>`, :ref:`IBTYPEE(k) <IBTYPEE>` ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=118]` ` Elevation boundary type, (At present the only allowable value for IBTYPEE is 0). Elevations are specified either in the Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File for harmonic forcing or in the Non-periodic Elevation Boundary Condition File for time series forcing. ` |[근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l33) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l33) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l33) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| F14-28 / 장벽 높이의 허용 등호 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=38]` ` External barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at this node, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARLANHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. `<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=28]` ` Internal barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j) and the paired node IBCONN(k,j)). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at these nodes, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j) and <b >BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(IBCONN(k,j)). If this is not satisfied, the run will terminate. ` |[근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l35) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l35) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/14-fort14-grid-bathymetry-boundaries.md#cvb-l35) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
| F14-29 / 내부 장벽 뒷면의 지형고 검사 | `[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=28]` ` Internal barrier height (positive above the geoid and negative below the geoid) at node NBVV(k,j) and the paired node IBCONN(k,j)). The barrier height must be greater than the bathymetric depth at these nodes, i.e., recalling the different sign convention between the bathymetric depth and the barrier height, BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(NBVV(k,j) and <b >BARINHT(k,j) > –DP(IBCONN(k,j)). | F20-01 / 읽기 조건 NFFR | `[file=docs/technical_reference/input_files/fort20.rst line=6]` `` The fort.20 file contains non-periodic, normal flow boundary conditions for "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary nodes. This file is only read when a "specified non-zero normal flow" boundary condition has been specified in the :doc:`Grid and Boundary Information File <fort14>` (IBTYPE=2, 12, or 22) and NFFR=0 in the :doc:`Model Parameter and Periodic Boundary Condition File <fort15>`. ``<br>`[file=docs/technical_reference/parameter_definitions/index.rst line=811]` ` Number of frequencies in the specified normal flow external boundary condition. If NFFR=0 or NFFR=-1, the normal flow boundary condition is assumed to be non-periodic and will be read in from the Non-periodic, Normal Flux Boundary Condition File. If NFFR=0, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the cold start time; but if NFFR=-1, ADCIRC assumes that the flux data in that file start at the hot start time. On the other hand, positive integer values of NFFR indicate the number of frequency components that make up the periodic flux boundaries. ` |[근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/17-boundary-and-forcing-inputs.md#cvb-l41) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/17-boundary-and-forcing-inputs.md#cvb-l41) | [근거·판정 정본](../manual-notes/17-boundary-and-forcing-inputs.md#cvb-l41) [10-12 결론 검증 보완] |
